# Unit 27 linguistic and owned-edition mapping packet

This packet prepares review. It records an unsigned structural mapping proposal. It does not record a qualified review, an approved edition alignment, or an install decision.

The owned-edition mapping worksheet separates observed edition facts, the proposed lesson and printed page range, unresolved edition questions, and matters that still require qualified linguistic review. ISBN, owned-copy verification, variance, reviewer identity, qualification, date, and approval stay blank. The disposition remains PENDING_CURRICULUM_REVIEWER.

Unit 27 remains in development. Its readiness is content-complete and review-pending. The Lesson 27 relationship is a proposed, unsigned mapping. It is not an approved edition alignment.

`mappingPackageDigest` binds the proposed edition-mapping facts for Unit 27: edition identity, lesson, printed page range, topic description, observation source, ISBN observation state, and the pending disposition. Reviewer identity, qualification, date, notes, correction notes, and signature stay outside that digest.

`packContentReviewDigest` is the repository aggregate of instructional curriculum. It is not an approval of this unit, and it is not part of the review-package digest.

`reviewPackageDigest` identifies this complete pending review package. It binds the ordered instructional records and the mapping-package digest. Changing a proposed page range or topic changes the mapping-package digest and therefore this package digest.

`repositorySourceDigest` binds repository and release inputs. Recording an approval later changes that repository hash. That change does not replace the signed content-review digest or the mapping-package digest.

kind=REMEDIATE
title=Remediation
body=Read the main verb and the participle separately. In Письмо, написанное Анной, лежит на столе., Анной identifies the writer. In Анна убрала прочитанное письмо., Анна put the letter away; прочитанное tells us it had been read but does not identify the reader.

language=en
prompt=Which admitted English label is written beside the shown form?

feedback=The frozen line says these forms are formed by means of the listed suffixes. This label is written beside the shown form. done/made.
objectiveId=obj-u27-passive
containsGeneratedRussian=false
reviewStatus=IMPLEMENTED_PENDING_FINAL_AUDIT
distractorRationales= | The frozen line writes this label beside a different form for written. | The frozen line writes this label beside a different form for killed.
testedFeature=PAST_PASSIVE
evidenceMode=RULE_CLASSIFIES_SOURCE_EXAMPLE
contrastSetId=u27-passive-0
cueLanguage=ru
independent=false

distractorSourceIds= | prov-ex-u27-passive-1-en | prov-ex-u27-passive-2-en

title=Units 26–30 source notice
summary=Proposed Units 26–30 adapt admitted CC BY-SA 4.0 spans. Unsupported topics stay in the copy you own. Bounded postal, stress, and dictionary tasks are review-pending.
detail=Wikipedia article text is frozen by oldid under CC BY-SA 4.0 via Wikipedia:Copyrights oldid 1369466282. The Wikibooks address note is frozen by oldid under the separate CC BY-SA 4.0 route recorded on Wikibooks:Copyrights oldid 4622060. The packaged CC BY-SA 4.0 legal code is cc-by-sa-4.0.txt. The adapted selection and arrangement are share-alike. Application code, user data, user recordings, and separately licensed audio are outside that statement. No audio is included. Linked media, Phonos, and pronunciation files are not admitted. Anecdotal postal advice is not shipped. Russian Wikipedia morphology oldid 148939075 is admitted only for the abbreviation declension contrast and the command-versus-supposition particle contrast, under the Russian Wikipedia copyrights oldid 154562152. Russian Wikipedia indefinite-pronoun page oldid 149963314 is admitted for the series meanings under that same Russian Wikipedia copyrights route. Russian Wikipedia participle page oldid 153078996 is admitted for the participial-phrase punctuation rule, including its exceptions, under that same route. Complete postal practice, participle stress, a general bookish-register claim, and dictionary use are not prepared. ReadyRussian letter conventions cover structure, the Russian and English organizational difference, and the stranger address limit. The abbreviation contrast and the particle contrast do not activate an authority variance. Review is pending. Bounded postal practice, past-passive stress, and a two-entry dictionary task are implemented and review-pending. They are not complete postal mastery, a general stress rule, or broad dictionary proficiency. Present-passive exploration does not gate Unit 27.

recordType=label
sourceUrl=urn:russian-companion:content-audit-research:2026-10-08
sourceRevision=content-audit-2026-10-08
sourceRecordId=ca-contrast:u27-i-comp-2
originalSourceText=Content-audit research note dated 2026-10-08. Bibliography includes the Gramota dictionary entries for некто and быть, the Gramota statement of vowels after hushers and ц, and Litnevskaya on the verbal adverb as search evidence for relative time. An imperfective form can report a completed event without asserting completion. Authorship: independently authored for Russian Companion. Status remains IMPLEMENTED_PENDING_FINAL_AUDIT. This note is not linguistic acceptance, mapping approval, installation, or owner acceptance. No owned-book prose, exercise, or answer key is copied.
Анна убрала прочитанное письмо.
Anna put away the letter that had been read.
Which reading keeps the reader of the letter unspecified?
прочитанное письмо means the letter that had been read. The sentence does not say that Anna read it.
The passive participle прочитанное does not name Anna as the reader.
Anna wrote the letter on the table
the letter put Anna away
Anna wrote the letter on the table invents a different verb.
the letter put Anna away reverses who did the action.

originalSourceText=Content-audit research note dated 2026-10-08. Bibliography includes the Gramota dictionary entries for некто and быть, the Gramota statement of vowels after hushers and ц, and Litnevskaya on the verbal adverb as search evidence for relative time. An imperfective form can report a completed event without asserting completion. Authorship: independently authored for Russian Companion. Status remains IMPLEMENTED_PENDING_FINAL_AUDIT. This note is not linguistic acceptance, mapping approval, installation, or owner acceptance. No owned-book prose, exercise, or answer key is copied.
Анна убрала прочитанное письмо.
Anna put away the letter that had been read.
Which reading keeps the reader of the letter unspecified?
прочитанное письмо means the letter that had been read. The sentence does not say that Anna read it.
The passive participle прочитанное does not name Anna as the reader.
Anna wrote the letter on the table
the letter put Anna away
Anna wrote the letter on the table invents a different verb.
the letter put Anna away reverses who did the action.
Анна убрала прочитанное письмо
прочитанное письмо
прочитанное

Анна убрала прочитанное письмо.
Anna put away the letter that had been read.
Which reading keeps the reader of the letter unspecified?
Анна убрала прочитанное письмо. means Anna put away the letter that had been read. Анна is the person who put it away; the passive participle does not identify who read it.
Anna wrote the letter on the table
the letter put Anna away
Anna wrote the letter on the table invents a different verb.
the letter put Anna away reverses who did the action.
Анна убрала прочитанное письмо
Анна

- Objectives: obj-u27-passive
- Sessions: u27-s-orient, u27-s-passive
- Source: src-wikipedia-russian-grammar-1369617627
- Rights: rights-wikipedia-russian-grammar-1369617627
- Classification: none
- Current review status: IMPLEMENTED_PENDING_FINAL_AUDIT
- Content hash: b21580b174f1b19a3895bd09f334c905f17a1e9a4896e16e800976203603389f

recordType=meaning
sourceUrl=https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Russian_grammar&oldid=1369617627
sourceRevision=1369617627
sourceRecordId=grammar::сде́лать – to do/to make (perfective verb)
:сде́ла'''нн'''ый – done/made

Passi
originalSourceText=:сде́лать – to do/to make (perfective verb)
:сде́ла'''нн'''ый – done/made

Passive past participles are formed by means of the suffixes ''''-нн-'''' or ''''-т-'''' from the infinitive stem of perfective verbs. Besides that, this kind of participle can have short forms formed by means of the suffixes ''''-н-'''' or ''''-т-'''':

{| class="wikitable"
| написа́ть ''(to write)'' – напи́са'''нн'''ый (written) / напи́са'''н''' (short form)
|}

{| class="wikitable"
| уби́ть ''(to kill)'' – уби́'''т'''ый (killed) / уби́'''т''' (short form)
shippedText=done/made
transformation=selection

originalSourceText=:обсужда́ть – to discuss;
:обсужда́емый (full form), обсужда́ем (short form) – being discussed ''or'' able to be discussed;

In order to form the passive present participle it is necessary to add an adjective ending to the 1st person plural of the present tense:

{| class="wikitable"

:обсужда́емый (full form), обсужда́ем (short form) – being discussed ''or'' able to be discussed;

In order to form the passive present participle it is necessary to add an adjective ending to the 1st person plural of the present tense:

{| class="wikitable"
| оставля́ть (to leave) – оставля́'''ем''' (we leave) – оставля́'''ем'''ый

recordType=original-synthesis
sourceUrl=urn:russian-companion:original-research-synthesis:phase3-2026-09-30
sourceRevision=phase3-2026-09-30
sourceRecordId=phase3:participle-comprehension
originalSourceText=Read the participial phrase for who did the action and what the result is.
Stress placement is a different objective and does not answer these readings.
Return to who wrote the letter and what Anna did after reading it.
Письмо, написанное Анной, лежит на столе.
the letter Anna wrote is on the table
Анна убрала прочитанное письмо.
Anna put away the letter she had read
Which English reading matches the letter that is on the table?
Admitted reading: the letter Anna wrote is on the table
Anna put the letter away
the table wrote the letter
Rejected reading: Anna put the letter away
Rejected reading: the table wrote the letter
Which English reading matches the person who produced the letter?
написанное Анной
Anna is the writer of the letter
Admitted reading: Anna is the writer of the letter
Anna is the reader of the letter
the letter wrote Anna
Rejected reading: Anna is the reader of the letter
Rejected reading: the letter wrote Anna
Which English reading matches what Anna did with the letter afterward?
Admitted reading: Anna put away the letter she had read
Anna wrote the letter on the table
the letter put Anna away
Rejected reading: Anna wrote the letter on the table
Rejected reading: the letter put Anna away
Which English reading matches the state of the letter Anna put away?
прочитанное письмо
the letter had already been read
Admitted reading: the letter had already been read
the letter had just been written
Anna is still reading aloud
Rejected reading: the letter had just been written
Rejected reading: Anna is still reading aloud
Письмо,
Письмо
написанное Анной,
лежит на столе.
лежит на столе
Анна убрала прочитанное письмо

- Objectives: obj-u27-stress

- Content hash: dc0c0d3c6ba15fdd9b7515ad21492750a9d9b9ebc731b80e5d78620364883c11

recordType=original-synthesis
sourceUrl=urn:russian-companion:original-research-synthesis:phase3-2026-09-30
sourceRevision=phase3-2026-09-30
sourceRecordId=phase3:participle-stress
originalSourceText=Only these four accent placements are admitted, and a different accent on the same letters is rejected.
Seeing an accent mark, or choosing a headword instead of an infinitive, does not test the placement.
These four placements do not generate stress for an unlisted participle.
Compare the admitted accent with the rejected accent on the same letters.
оставля́емый
admitted stress placement
напи́санный
Which stress placement is admitted for the leave-behind present passive?
the present passive of оставлять
Admitted reading: оставля́емый
оставляемы́й
Rejected reading: оставляемы́й
Which stress placement is admitted for the discuss present passive?
the present passive of обсуждать
обсужда́емый
Admitted reading: обсужда́емый
обсуждаемы́й
Rejected reading: обсуждаемы́й
Which stress placement is admitted for the write past passive?
the past passive of написать
Admitted reading: напи́санный
написа́нный
Rejected reading: написа́нный
Which stress placement is admitted for the read past passive?
the past passive of прочитать
прочи́танный
Admitted reading: прочи́танный
прочита́нный
Rejected reading: прочита́нный
Which English description states the limit of these four stress placements?
an unlisted participle
no stress rule is admitted beyond these four
Admitted reading: no stress rule is admitted beyond these four
these four placements generate every other participle
Rejected reading: these four placements generate every other participle
оставлять
обсуждать
написать
прочитать
shippedText=Only these four accent placements are admitted, and a different accent on the same letters is rejected.

licenseUrl=urn:russian-companion:license:original-app-text

shareAlikeTreatment=Original research synthesis authored for this companion. It is not a CC BY-SA quotation. Application code, user data, and user recordings are outside this statement.

- Objectives: obj-u27-passive
- Sessions: u27-s-orient, u27-s-passive
- Source: src-wikipedia-russian-grammar-1369617627
- Rights: rights-wikipedia-russian-grammar-1369617627
- Classification: none
- Current review status: IMPLEMENTED_PENDING_FINAL_AUDIT
- Content hash: d71f2c5501db865bfc84eae360dfc947268d2997e3af07f5cd083b39e3fa5798

recordType=instruction
sourceUrl=https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=Russian_grammar&oldid=1369617627
sourceRevision=1369617627
sourceRecordId=grammar::сде́лать – to do/to make (perfective verb)
:сде́ла'''нн'''ый – done/made

Passi
originalSourceText=:сде́лать – to do/to make (perfective verb)
:сде́ла'''нн'''ый – done/made

Passive past participles are formed by means of the suffixes ''''-нн-'''' or ''''-т-'''' from the infinitive stem of perfective verbs. Besides that, this kind of participle can have short forms formed by means of the suffixes ''''-н-'''' or ''''-т-'''':

{| class="wikitable"
| написа́ть ''(to write)'' – напи́са'''нн'''ый (written) / напи́са'''н''' (short form)
|}

sourceId=src-content-audit-research
sourceRevision=content-audit-2026-10-08
sourceRecordId=ca-corrected-capability:cap-obj-u27-comprehension
sourceHash=30e63fa8b8357c075e51ec64285936905a62e8acc627099b340f4d16f38da202
supportedClaim=participle-comprehension
exactSpan=Pending corrected research applications. No linguistic or owner approval is recorded.
References: retained original source bindings in this unit; the independent content-audit register; Gramota dictionary and orthography; Litnevskaya, verbal adverbs; Rusgram, verbal adverbs.
https://gramota.ru/poisk?query=быстрый&mode=slovari
https://gramota.ru/biblioteka/spravochniki/russkij-yazyk-kratkij-teoreticheskij-kurs-dlya-shkolnikov/deeprichastie
Read the participial phrase for who did the action and what the result is.
Stress placement is a different objective and does not answer these readings.
Read the main verb and the participle separately. In Письмо, написанное Анной, лежит на столе., Анной identifies the writer. In Анна убрала прочитанное письмо., Анна put the letter away; прочитанное tells us it had been read but does not identify the reader.
Письмо, написанное Анной, лежит на столе.
the letter Anna wrote is on the table
Анна убрала прочитанное письмо.
Anna put away the letter that had been read.
написанное Анной
Anna is the writer of the letter
Which English reading matches the person who produced the letter?
Admitted reading: Anna is the writer of the letter
Anna is the reader of the letter
the letter wrote Anna
Rejected reading: Anna is the reader of the letter
Rejected reading: the letter wrote Anna
Which reading keeps the reader of the letter unspecified?
Анна убрала прочитанное письмо. means Anna put away the letter that had been read. Анна is the person who put it away; the passive participle does not identify who read it.
Anna wrote the letter on the table
the letter put Anna away
Anna wrote the letter on the table invents a different verb.
the letter put Anna away reverses who did the action.
прочитанное письмо
the letter had already been read
Which English reading matches the state of the letter Anna put away?
Admitted reading: the letter had already been read
the letter had just been written
Anna is still reading aloud
Rejected reading: the letter had just been written
Rejected reading: Anna is still reading aloud

- Content hash: ffb618ecae16c0b129eef0142a589f90cca507451a644db16a9f6dc83919cf9c

sourceId=src-wikipedia-russian-grammar-1369617627
sourceRevision=1369617627
sourceRecordId=grammar::сде́лать – to do/to make (perfective verb)
:сде́ла'''нн'''ый – done/made

Passi
sourceHash=d5d52caab6dbed44f28ad3781309cb45b3437e19e891124b10dda641eadccf7c
supportedClaim=passive-participle
exactSpan=:сде́лать – to do/to make (perfective verb)
:сде́ла'''нн'''ый – done/made

Passive past participles are formed by means of the suffixes ''''-нн-'''' or ''''-т-'''' from the infinitive stem of perfective verbs. Besides that, this kind of participle can have short forms formed by means of the suffixes ''''-н-'''' or ''''-т-'''':

{| class="wikitable"
| написа́ть ''(to write)'' – напи́са'''нн'''ый (written) / напи́са'''н''' (short form)
|}

{| class="wikitable"
| уби́ть ''(to kill)'' – уби́'''т'''ый (killed) / уби́'''т''' (short form)

sourceId=src-original-research-synthesis
sourceRevision=phase3-2026-09-30
sourceRecordId=phase3:participle-stress
sourceHash=e4dad2bc4f594d302dce65ba8a62029020023ef247c0984eb98378b6784f53a7
supportedClaim=participle-stress
exactSpan=Only these four accent placements are admitted, and a different accent on the same letters is rejected.
Seeing an accent mark, or choosing a headword instead of an infinitive, does not test the placement.
These four placements do not generate stress for an unlisted participle.
Compare the admitted accent with the rejected accent on the same letters.
оставля́емый
admitted stress placement
напи́санный
Which stress placement is admitted for the leave-behind present passive?
the present passive of оставлять
Admitted reading: оставля́емый
оставляемы́й
Rejected reading: оставляемы́й
Which stress placement is admitted for the discuss present passive?
the present passive of обсуждать
обсужда́емый
Admitted reading: обсужда́емый
обсуждаемы́й
Rejected reading: обсуждаемы́й
Which stress placement is admitted for the write past passive?
the past passive of написать
Admitted reading: напи́санный
написа́нный
Rejected reading: написа́нный
Which stress placement is admitted for the read past passive?
the past passive of прочитать
прочи́танный
Admitted reading: прочи́танный
прочита́нный
Rejected reading: прочита́нный
Which English description states the limit of these four stress placements?
an unlisted participle
no stress rule is admitted beyond these four
Admitted reading: no stress rule is admitted beyond these four
these four placements generate every other participle
Rejected reading: these four placements generate every other participle
оставлять
обсуждать
написать
прочитать
